[chromecast] Fix dangling raw_ptr in AudioSocketBroker

Remove the unused socket_broker_ member variable and stop passing the
unretained this pointer across sequence boundaries in SequenceBound,
preventing PartitionAlloc dangling pointer crashes when
AudioSocketBroker is destroyed before the background socket connection
completes.

Bug: b:514123245, b:514401084
Test: cast_shell_unittests --gtest_filter=AudioSocketBrokerTest.*

[chromecast] Fix ThreadSafeReactorHandle UAF in heartbeat streaming

This fixes a session teardown Use-After-Free (UAF) crash affecting
Android-based Cast receivers by making the heartbeat reactor thread-safe
and managing its lifecycle properly using ThreadSafeReactorHandle.

[net] Remove the expired Net.CertVerifier.Mac* histograms

TrustStoreMac recorded seven histograms about its trust and keychain
certificate caches: Net.CertVerifier.MacTrustDomainCertCount.{Domain},
MacTrustDomainCacheInitTime, MacTrustImplCacheInitTime and
MacKeychainCerts.{IntermediateCacheInitTime,IntermediateCount,
TotalCount,TrustCount}. All of them expired in 2024 (2024-02-01 or
2024-04-28), so the code that records them is dead.

Remove the three recording helpers, their call sites and the timers
that only fed them, the now-unused includes, the histogram expectations
in TrustStoreMacImplTest.SystemCerts, and the histograms.xml entries.
The cache behavior is unchanged.

R=mattm@chromium.org

Fixed: 41485528, 41485529
Bug: 330166367

> [Autofill][Clank] Separate LegalMessageLines with paragraph break

[chromecast] Fix NPE in CastWebContentsIntentUtils with null extras

When an intent without extras is passed to CastWebContentsIntentUtils,
intent.getExtras() returns null. Dereferencing getExtras() directly
causes a NullPointerException crash.

Check intent.getExtras() before access to handle null extras safely.

Bug: b:482137889
Test: cast_shell_junit_tests

[chromecast] Embed APK version as Product Version in Crashpad

This modifies the Cast crash reporter client to explicitly set the
Crashpad product_info->version to the APK package version name
(e.g. 3.74.x) instead of the Chromium release string. To ensure
ApkInfo.getPackageVersionName() returns the host APK version rather than
falling back to VersionInfo.getProductVersion(), this also calls
ApkInfo.setBrowserPackageInfo(packageInfo) in
CastBrowserApplication.initialize().

This allows the crash backend to seamlessly cluster native Crashpad
dumps alongside Java exception logs (which already use the APK version).
The original Chromium release string is preserved via a new
browser_version crash key.

Additionally, we now explicitly override the ptype crash key for the
core process vs the sandboxed browser process on Android, separating
cast_service from cast_browser in crash reports since both natively
initialize Crashpad as ptype: browser.

Bug: b:553039596
Test: Verified on-device crash uploads for cast_service and cast_browser

614e7aa650d5f602afee79caa86c595526fe247b [ diff ] 
Make redundant BASE_FEATURE and BASE_FEATURE_PARAM macros a presubmit error

Update CheckBaseFeatureMacro and CheckBaseFeatureParamMacro in
PRESUBMIT.py to emit a PresubmitError instead of a
PresubmitPromptWarning when a 3-argument BASE_FEATURE /
BASE_RUNTIME_MUTABLE_FEATURE, 5-argument BASE_FEATURE_PARAM, or
6-argument BASE_FEATURE_ENUM_PARAM macro is used and the C++ identifier
matches 'k' + the string literal (e.g. BASE_FEATURE(kFoo, "Foo", ...)).
In this case the explicit string literal form is completely redundant
with the shorter form, and a warning is often bypassed in
non-interactive or AI-assisted workflows.

Keep the existing PresubmitPromptWarning when the C++ identifier and
string literal differ (noting the preference for identical spelling), or
when the identifier does not start with 'k' followed by an uppercase
letter.

Bug: 436274260

d6264ec2b9055c70853aa903ed6690dd81172a9d
parent
de174284cfe08e3dc03b7d41978e68c25f047cc7 [ diff ] 
bindings: Statically resolve member [Exposed] on non-[Global] interfaces

On non-[Global] interfaces, bind_gen currently treats any member with an
[Exposed] attribute (such as one inherited from a mixin or partial
interface) as context-dependent, generating a runtime check like
execution_context->IsWindow() in InstallContextDependentProperties().

When the member's [Exposed] already covers every global scope where the
enclosing interface is exposed (e.g. an [Exposed=Window] mixin member on
Navigator, which is itself [Exposed=Window]), this runtime check is
redundant. It also prevents the property from being installed on
wrappers created in detached contexts, as V8DOMWrapper::CreateWrapper()
only installs unconditional properties when no execution context is
available.

Resolve [Exposed] names to their concrete [Global] interfaces so that
members whose exposure is a superset of their enclosing interface's
exposure are installed unconditionally (or context-independently when
gated on a runtime-enabled feature).

This drops redundant global checks in generated bindings and exposes
navigator.mediaSession on detached windows, updating the
property-access-on-cached-window-* baselines accordingly.

Bug: 568359881, 470288157

[MChips] Add option button on the composeplate layout.

This CL adds an optional button to the composeplate row on NTPs, between the AI Mode (composeplate) button and the incognito button.
The button is hidden by default. In this CL, we update the UI of the AI
mode row for mobiles:
- When the optional button is visible:
  - The incognito button hides its text and wraps its content.
  - The composeplate button keeps filling the remaining width.
  - Lateral padding of the incognito and optional icon:
    - Portrait: 32dp, landscape: 40dp.
  - Margin between each button: portrait: 4dp, landscape: 12dp.
  - Lateral padding compared with fake search box:
    - portrait: 16dp (unchanged), landscape: 32dp.
- When it is hidden, the original layout is restored.
Since the button has different lateral padding on landscape mode, NewTabPageCoordinator forwards display style changes to
ComposeplateCoordinator on phones, which re-reads the dimensions so the
landscape values are applied.
Screenshot: http://shortn/_1MDTsaNQfy.

Bug: 568005713

Raise renderer FD limit to 4096 on ChromeOS and Linux
📋 Metadata
commit
b10b0e3012deb9cd3b1237d8f48dde27158af7f2
[ log ]
author
vikas soni <vikassoni@chromium.org>
Sat Oct 03 02:25:25 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Sat Oct 03 02:25:25 2026
tree
6cc3f6afb86a2fd5c8cf9995086ea57f025694fa
parent
396fdadeead7128af73055f6f2e44b3c53d5b179 [ diff ] 
Raise renderer FD limit to 4096 on ChromeOS and Linux

With MappableSI, renderer processes hold dma-buf FDs for native pixmap
GpuMemoryBufferHandles in addition to the GPU process. On ChromeOS, the
renderer process inherits the default 2048 soft FD limit from the
zygote, which can be exhausted when many video decoders are active.

Call base::IncreaseFdLimitTo() in
RendererMainPlatformDelegate::EnableSandbox() to raise the renderer soft
FD limit to at least 4096 before initializing the sandbox.

Bug: 563801582, 404365358

parent
e7b6e29d582113b26a7d365e7f242c7d32f6d4cd [ diff ] 
Ensure image_capture_mojolpm_fuzzer has a MediaStreamManager

The existing test harness setup didn't actually create a
MediaStreamManager so the tests could fail flakily with a null pointer
dereference when attempting to access it. Fix the implementation to not
depend on BrowserMainLoop (the MediaStreamManager is available directly
on the IO thread) and initialize it with fake devices in the fuzz test.

Fixed: 565641934

author

tree
64d40d49692b9c384b177cf1089fbdeaf789a322
parent
d85d969197d0e004e21fb6d01308e4971e711422 [ diff ] 
[ash] Remove the expired graphics tablet pen button trimming

GraphicsTabletPrefHandlerImpl::InitializeGraphicsTabletSettings() ran
TrimButtonRemappingListForGraphicsTabletPen() on every initialization
to drop pen buttons that incorrect metadata had added for some graphics
tablets. The result is written back to prefs right away, so each
tablet only needed it once, and the helper was marked "Remove after
07/2025 (M139) as this trimming will no longer be needed".

The tree is now well past M139. Remove the helper, its call, and the
two tests that only covered it.

R=michaelcheco@google.com

Bug: None

tree
547ea2a6343178b3de6b41d7642f16abaff469b7
parent
525245df8ff276bccd565cb35279399e59427da6 [ diff ] 
Mac: Activate the widget when focusing a View in an inactive widget

FocusManager::SetFocusedViewWithReason() activates an inactive widget
before focusing a View in it, so that focus implies activation. This was
added in 2016 (codereview.chromium.org/2371113003), but Mac was excluded
only because some AccessiblePaneViewTests failed there, with a TODO
pointing at crbug.com/40486728. As a result, on Mac a View can have
focus in a window that isn't key, so keystrokes keep going to another
window (e.g. a tab-modal dialog). Various Mac-only workarounds have
accumulated for this, such as ActivateWindowAfterFocusCommand() in
browser_commands.cc.

This CL removes the Mac exclusion, behind the kill switch
kMacActivateWidgetOnFocusRequest (enabled by default):

- NativeWidgetPrivate::ShouldActivateOnFocusRequest() lets the platform
  decide whether to activate. It returns true by default, keeping the
  existing behavior on other platforms.
- On Mac, it returns true only when activation is synchronous and has no
  side effects beyond making the window key: the window is in-process,
  visible, can become key, and the app is already active. This avoids
  showing hidden windows, stealing focus from other apps, and async
  activation of out-of-process (app shim) windows. Otherwise, the View
  is focused without activating, as before.
- NativeWidgetMacNSWindowHost::OnIsFirstResponderChanged() no longer
  restores the focused View when the window isn't key. Such a first
  responder change is programmatic (e.g. ClearNativeFocus() during a
  tab switch), and restoring focus would now activate the window. Focus
  is restored when the window becomes key.
- ActivateWindowAfterFocusCommand() in browser_commands.cc is skipped;
  focus commands now activate the browser window through FocusManager.
- ToastController's Mac-only OnWidgetActivationChanged() workarounds
  (clearing the toast's stored focus view on activation and the
  browser's focus on deactivation) are skipped. They compensated for
  focus being set before activation; with activation now going through
  the stored focus view, clearing it left the toast with no focus.
- BrowserView::OnActiveTabChanged() now only restores focus on a tab
  change if the widget is active, as on other platforms. The Mac-only
  exception (crbug.com/926492) relied on restoring focus not activating
  the widget. Otherwise a tab change in an inactive browser, e.g. when
  attaching a dragged tab, would synchronously activate it. Focus is
  restored on activation instead (restore_focus_on_activation_).

The skipped workarounds are kept so that disabling the kill switch
restores the previous behavior. They can be removed together with the
kill switch.

parent
80ce3a67e8b40457ac1cee5aa6a59157acb173ae [ diff ] 
idle: Reject opaque origins in IdleManagerImpl::HasPermission

When resolving permissions for IDLE_DETECTION, opaque origins (such as
sandboxed frames or uncommitted initial popup documents) can fall back
to WebContents::GetVisibleURL() in PermissionUtil. This can resolve
against an uncommitted pending entry's origin rather than the opaque
origin itself, allowing an attacker to borrow a victim origin's
persisted permission grant.

This CL explicitly returns false in IdleManagerImpl::HasPermission() if
the requesting frame's last committed origin is opaque, preventing
cross-origin permission inheritance and grant enumeration.

9042c2563552ecc0deabeac1d4eeb3a459999d5d
parent
c66e065868475e2b2802d4bf71a3e8bdd2c4d8b5 [ diff ] 
idle-detection: Sever idle monitors upon permission revocation

When a user revokes the IDLE_DETECTION permission in site settings,
IdleManagerImpl continues delivering user idle and screen lock state
updates to existing documents. This happens because IdleManagerImpl
only checks permission at monitor registration and does not subscribe
to permission changes via PermissionController.

This CL subscribes IdleManagerImpl to permission status changes using
PermissionController::SubscribeToPermissionResultChange while any
monitor is registered. When permission is revoked, all active monitor
pipes are dropped, polling is stopped and the subscription is released.

[ContextualTasks] Remove handshake check from pin button visibility

The side panel pin/unpin button in the Contextual Tasks overflow menu
only updates PinnedToolbarActionsModel on the browser Profile via
ContextualTasksToolbarPageHandler and does not interact with the AIM
webview or postMessage handshake. Removing `this.isHandshakeComplete`
from `shouldShowPinButton_()` allows the pin button to be shown on AI
pages without waiting for the webview handshake and removes the unused
`isHandshakeComplete` property from the toolbar and overflow menu.

Also update `baseline_cl` to "8500700" for ContextualTasksToolbarMenu
in ContextualTasksToolbarPixelTest since the pin menu item is now
visible in the overflow menu on AI pages.

bluetooth: Clean up active notify sessions on ForgetDevice

tree
804dda1fe806160f7d143ee828d3949b1f086e60
parent
dce5568fd87d589fbce579d888284185c5edbb70 [ diff ] 
bluetooth: Clean up active notify sessions on ForgetDevice

WebBluetoothServiceImpl::ForgetDevice() revoked device access on the
legacy permissions backend by removing the device address from
allowed_devices(). However, it did not tear down active GATT notify
sessions, pending connection requests, or watch advertisement clients
associated with the forgotten device. If the physical connection was
kept open by another frame or origin, BLE characteristic notification
events continued to be dispatched to the renderer.

This CL introduces CleanupDeviceState() and invokes it during
ForgetDevice() when running on the legacy permissions backend. It closes
the GATT connection, cancels pending connection attempts, removes watch
advertisement clients, and drains in-flight and deferred notify session
requests with GATT_NOT_AUTHORIZED.

Prefer staged Android-synced theme in CrossDeviceSettingImporter

Before maybeApplyBackgroundUpdateFromDeviceSync() runs, an
Android-synced theme is staged in getSyncedNtpBackgroundData() while
getNtpBackgroundData() holds stale or null state. Without preferring the
staged theme, cross-OS imports diff against stale state and Undo reverts
to the old/default theme instead of the user's Android-synced theme.

202f47259e5cc9adc41062873eec0d4aac37b390 [ diff ] 
[glic] Fix Android mic permission flow not showing

With GlicNoWebview, GlicPwcPermissionDelegate decides Gemini's mic
permission, which skips Chrome's usual prompt for a missing Android
RECORD_AUDIO permission. Tapping the mic then failed silently: with the
Gemini mic setting off it was denied, and with the setting on but the
Android permission revoked, capture failed with no prompt or snackbar.

To make GlicNoWebview behave like the webview, which always allows mic
requests on all platforms:
- GlicPwcPermissionDelegate always grants MEDIASTREAM_MIC. This also
  applies to desktop GlicNoWebview, matching desktop webview.
- After the user accepts Chrome's mic dialog, the Android permission is
  requested directly instead of through MediaCaptureDevicesDispatcher.
  If it is denied, the mic disabled snackbar is shown. If the activity
  is destroyed before Android answers, the request is denied.
- The Android mic permission UI is owned by GlicKeyedService so tests
  can replace it.

Adds an Android browser test for the flow with and without
GlicNoWebview.

Follow-ups: b/568819844 (Gemini mic setting doesn't gate mic access),
b/568819419 (same issue for location), b/568870385 (reuse Chrome's
standard Android permission reprompt instead of a Glic-specific one).

TAG=agy
CONV=83375f7a-30f4-49a1-92ae-9acc4ebee8ba

[base/i18n] Bind DateTimeFormatter instances to a locale
📋 Metadata

202f47259e5cc9adc41062873eec0d4aac37b390
[ log ]
author

Sat Oct 03 00:57:20 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Sat Oct 03 00:57:20 2026
tree
6ea30a51bdd8bc44838a5fe1a59eab8fe2ec5c41
parent
3d5fe7b59ba3e4ba58516fd9eacc61107c4afa2f [ diff ] 
[base/i18n] Bind DateTimeFormatter instances to a locale

DateTimeFormatter now receives the locale in its constructor and uses it
to format when no explicit locale is passed to Format().

IcuBridge owns one formatter per locale, cached in an
absl::flat_hash_map keyed by LanguageTag, and exposes:
  - date_time_formatter(): returns the formatter for the current default
    ICU locale, obtained from GetDefaultIcuLocale().
  - date_time_formatter(locale): returns the formatter for the given
    locale.

Formatters are created on first use. The map is lazily populated by the
const accessors and guarded by a lock, as IcuBridge is a process-wide
singleton that can be accessed from any thread.

This change is being done so a cache can be more conveniently
implemented for improving the performance of DateTimeFormatter.

8ef8c2943a3c44f28d9bb95a00421ca4d10c58d2 [ diff ] 
[blinkpy] Remove redundant default_expectations_files() overrides

AndroidPort and WebviewPort overrides default_expectations_files(). As
crrev.com/c/6520154 removed MobileTestExpectations, both overrides
return the same list as Port.default_expectations_files(), so this
removes them.

All callers iterate the result once or wrap it in list()/set(), so
returning the base class's filter object instead of a list makes no
difference.

It also removes the unused get_blink_dir import from android.py.

No behavior change.

eaa1e50590c9cf6161a1ef5a13131236a704afbc
parent
e73026973e50a9b8ff8a3cb14d8f196ba334f184 [ diff ] 
[UIA] Fix selection lookup for focused iframes

Repeated UIA word selection inside a focused iframe can stall or fail to
contract when Narrator scan mode is enabled. Narrator's Scan Mode uses
UIA range operations. The queried provider belongs to the outer
document, but the actual selection lives in the article iframe's
accessibility tree. An empty or projected outer-document range makes
subsequent movement and selection target the wrong tree, and incorrectly
resets the anchor.

To fix, we resolve selection through a focused descendant in a known,
different accessibility tree. Normalize only redirected endpoints to
leaf text positions, preserving same-tree behavior.

Addressing this has uncovered an additional UIA issue, where a selection
in the outer document when using UIA word selection up to the end of the
framed content. This is tracked via https://crbug.com/568852325.

715470f6edc3b18eac44016e23fc7c96dfa0fc0b [ diff ] 
[Reading Mode] Don't turn unlinked anchors into links on toggle

Avoid setting data-link or href when a link has an empty URL, and strip
href from unmatched Readability anchors before converting them to spans
so toggling links off and back on does not turn them into links.

This is already done when building the tree the first time when links
are enabled. This CL just ensures it's done when toggling off and on
again.

📋 Metadata

tree
721363f22607e197b2c30a7699f58ff96d6635c6
parent
05ad0ebee3c88d983b90659a718012f7605c784c [ diff ] 
[NTP Customization Sync] Plumb the attribution action URL to sync

When a theme collection image is applied by rebuilding the background
preference, i.e. when it is re-selected from the local theme history or
imported from another device, Android wrote an empty attribution action
URL and pushed that to sync. Desktop then lost the link on the
attribution, and iOS applied the synced background without it.

Carry the URL along with the two attribution lines:
- CustomBackgroundInfo gets a non-null attributionActionUrl, which is an
  empty GURL when there is no link. Like the attribution lines it
  describes the image rather than identifies it, so it is not part of
  equals().
- The JNI factories, the cross-device theme tracker and the theme
  history (from CollectionImage.attributionUrl) fill it in.
- onThemeCommitted() passes it down to
  UpdateThemeCollectionPrefsWithColor(), which writes it instead of an
  empty GURL.
- The theme history JSON stores it under "attributionActionUrl" when it
  is not empty.

Bug: 488439751

69f9503a321dc8d8aa1c2d0566d4b6d4048f5d75 [ diff ] 
Geolocation: Cache permission denied error in OnClientsChanged()

Ensure GeolocationProviderImpl::OnClientsChanged() populates the cached
permission denied error result (result_) when system_permission_status_
is kDenied.

Previously, OnClientsChanged() returned early without populating
result_ when system_permission_status_ was kDenied. If
GeolocationProviderImpl was lazily instantiated while system permission
was already denied (or after all previous clients disconnected and
cleared result_), AddLocationUpdateCallback() did not invoke the newly
registered callback with an error, leaving GeolocationImpl's
QueryNextPosition() request pending indefinitely.

d42993349409f940f66404060a2f560a26b23f2e [ diff ] 
[Crubit] Parallelize Crubit actions via metadata groups

Previously, `cc_bindings_from_rs` (Crubit) actions had a build-time
dependency on `_rspgen_target_name` (`generated_file`), which in turn
depended on `_original_attributes.deps` (e.g. `//build/rust/std:std_bindings`).
Because `std_bindings` and its constituent targets depend on upstream
Crubit header generation actions, this caused GN to emit Ninja hard
dependencies serializing all Crubit actions (e.g. `signed_web_bundles`
behind `std_bindings`, `std_bindings` behind `alloc_bindings`).

`cc_bindings_from_rs` only needs the GN metadata (header path strings,
namespaces, and features) written into the `.rsp` file at `gn gen` time.
It does not consume the generated C++ header files at runtime.

This CL introduces a pure metadata target `_metadata_target_name`
(`${target_name}_crubit_metadata`) including `std_bindings_crubit_metadata`
that contains only GN metadata without any action or library dependencies.
`_rspgen_target_name` depends on these metadata targets instead of the
action-bearing targets, and `_action_target_name` explicitly lists
`_action_rsp_path` in its `inputs` and depends on `_rspgen_target_name`.

This:
1. Breaks the Ninja harddeps chain between Crubit actions, allowing
   all Crubit actions to run fully concurrently as soon as their Rust
   libraries build.
2. Ensures `_action_rsp_path` is explicitly declared in `inputs` so
   remote execution (RBE) workers receive the `.rsp` file.
3. Preserves `_original_attributes.deps` on `source_set(_h_target_name)`
   so that downstream C++ compilations still wait for generated headers.

317d159df9030c74236eb5be77650a8ffc157dec [ diff ] 
blink/dom: Add RAW_PTR_EXCLUSION and STACK_ALLOCATED annotations

Covers core/dom.

Marks stack-allocated cache scope pointers in Document and static table
fields in EventTarget with RAW_PTR_EXCLUSION.

Also converts QualifiedNameComponents and QualifiedNameData from
DISALLOW_NEW() to STACK_ALLOCATED() because they are only instantiated
on the stack (as temporary lookup keys for AddWithTranslator and
HashComponents; QualifiedNameCache stores QualifiedNameImpl* rather than
QualifiedNameData, and HashComponents requires unique object
representations via base::byte_span_from_ref).

tree
f1978781861ffe36a38a387756f95ce12fe3d496
parent
fe68f83038cccffc1169f87c54c7349a18bc03a2 [ diff ] 
Identify ComposeboxMenuSharedTab by input item identifier

Replace `serverToken` with `inputItemIdentifier` on
`ComposeboxMenuSharedTab` and its removal delegate/mediator methods
(`didRemoveTabWithInputItemIdentifier:` and
`removeSharedTabWithInputItemIdentifier:`).

`ComposeboxMenuSharedTab` is a UI view model for the Shared Tabs menu in
Cobrowse. It only needs to identify the corresponding
`ComposeboxInputItem` in `ComposeboxInputPlateMediator` when the user
removes a shared tab. Using the local `ComposeboxInputItem.identifier`
(which exists immediately upon item creation) instead of `serverToken`
(which is only populated once a server upload starts) decouples the UI
menu from the server upload lifecycle and prepares for lazily uploading
auto-added tabs.

a8c0f12b674ed856aea09d24a0486b4102cfce8a
parent
39204b5379bbfec0d0d7ba8bef7690b1c7f4b11a [ diff ] 
[glic] Attach onboarding status and arm request headers to guest requests

When loading the Glic guest web client (gemini.google.com/glic), the
server-side rendering (SSR) module renders an initial input area by
default. For users in the first run experience (FRE), rendering this
input area causes visual jank before client-side hydration hides or
transitions the UI.

The web-side previously suppressed the input area by checking if the
request URL path was /glic/fre. However, onboarding arm 2 uses the
root /glic path instead, leaving the server without a signal to know
whether the request is for an onboarding user or a consented user.

This CL injects X-Glic-Onboarding-Completed (true/false) and
X-Glic-Onboarding-Arm (2 when incomplete) on Glic guest requests. The
headers are injected across both NoWebview (via GlicURLLoaderThrottle
and GlicSubresourceProxyingURLLoaderFactory) and Webview (via
GlicRequestHeaderInjector) modes. Both headers are also added to the
CORS-exempt list so they survive cross-origin and redirect requests.

Unit and browser tests are added to verify the header injection across
both consented and non-consented profiles in both execution modes.

blink/layout: Convert raw pointer fields to UnprotectedInRelease

Covers core/layout.

These fields get no BRP protection in release builds (the trait resolves
to RawPtrNoOpImpl, matching the previous raw pointer's cost). This is a
first step to enable dangling pointer detection on fuzzers and tests,
and we plan to convert suitable fields to regular protected raw_ptr in
the future.

Also marks union fields in InkOverflow with RAW_PTR_EXCLUSION.

Fields converted: 3 across 3 files (plus 2 fields marked
RAW_PTR_EXCLUSION in 1 file).

[WebAuthn] Add support for multiple credentials to Ambient bubble

This change:
- Allows the ambient bubble to receive a vector of credentials for
  display.
- The first credential is still initially displayed in the bubble with
  the "Sign in" button.
- There is now an 'unfold' icon that expands the bubble when clicked.
- The expanded bubble will have hover icons for all credentials, and
  no "Sign in" button. Clicking the credential selects it.

The unfold icon is still present even when there is only one
credential. It removes the Sign in button in this case, allowing
more area to see the un-elided credential.

treat it as a load error rather than a successful navigation. This
prevents the panel from sitting in the loading state until the timeout.

tree
698ad98eb978dd51a78d479043a036665790b6c3
parent
79367b82f769ed8dcdfd295f9c3a409190e82197 [ diff ] 
gamepad: Support Switch Pro 48-byte Bluetooth input reports

Nintendo Switch Pro Controllers and Joy-Cons connected over Bluetooth
transmit 49-byte HID transfers (1-byte report ID + 48-byte report body)
for standard input reports (0x30) and SPI subcommand replies (0x21),
whereas USB connections transfer 64 bytes (1-byte ID + 63-byte body).

Previously, crrev.com/c/7694899 introduced input report size checks
validating reports against 63-byte USB structures (ControllerDataReport
and SpiReadReport). On Linux, where hidraw delivers exact Bluetooth
packet sizes rather than Windows HID subsystem-padded reports, this
caused all incoming Bluetooth reports to be rejected, breaking device
initialization and gamepad input polling.

This CL resolves the issue by:
1. Defining ControllerDataReportBluetooth (48 bytes) and
   SpiReadReportHeader (19 bytes) packed structs to model the Bluetooth
   report layout.
2. Checking bus_type_ to dynamically validate 48-byte minimum sizes on
   Bluetooth and 63-byte sizes on USB in HandleInputReport30,
   HandleInputReport21, and ContinueInitSequence.
3. Updating touched report handler lines from UNSAFE_TODO to
   UNSAFE_BUFFERS with explicit // SAFETY: comments documenting size
   invariants.
4. Updating SPI read unpacking to accept base::span<const uint8_t> with
   defensive bounds checking against the actual payload size.

TAG=agy
CONV=84dfcb4c-c40a-40a5-bf4b-4058eaa22aa5

[Glic] Extract Android form factor check and add unit tests

GlicGlobalEnabling::IsSystemRequirementMet() skips its Android form
factor check when switches::kTestType is set and caches its result in a
function-local static, so the check had no unit test coverage.

Move the check into GlicGlobalEnabling::IsAndroidFormFactorAllowed(),
which takes the form factor and whether the device is foldable as
parameters. IsSystemRequirementMet() passes ui::GetDeviceFormFactor()
and base::android::device_info::is_foldable(), so behavior is unchanged.

Add unit tests for phones, desktop, tablets with and without
kGlicAndroidTablet, and folded and unfolded foldables.

Follow-up to https://crrev.com/c/8497766.

TAG=agy

blink/editing: Convert raw pointer fields to UnprotectedInRelease

Covers core/editing.

These fields get no BRP protection in release builds (the trait resolves
to RawPtrNoOpImpl, matching the previous raw pointer's cost). This is a
first step to enable dangling pointer detection on fuzzers and tests,
and we plan to convert suitable fields to regular protected raw_ptr in
the future.

Also marks static table fields with RAW_PTR_EXCLUSION and stack-only
helper ScopedHandlingInputEvent as STACK_ALLOCATED() because it is only
instantiated on the stack.

Fields converted: 2 in 1 file (plus 2 fields marked RAW_PTR_EXCLUSION
across 2 files and 1 class marked STACK_ALLOCATED()).

a7301ae1fdcc29886cc2d8ffea612382d650bff3
parent
dde8a5edeea126385be1456d8198025f1263ee67 [ diff ] 
[APC] Add label sources and remove repeated text

Capture label text that actionable APC currently misses. When ARIA
provides no text, try HTML title, image-button alt or value, CSS
alternative text, SVG title or desc, SVG link xlink:title, and table
summary. An explicitly empty CSS alternative suppresses fallback labels.

Remove a label if it exactly matches one extracted text node within its
element's subtree or its image caption. Comparisons trim surrounding
whitespace and preserve case. They use the text already extracted for
APC, avoiding another read of each labelled DOM subtree. Matches stay
within the same frame.

`AIPageContentLabelExpandFallbackSources` enables the new label sources
by default. `AIPageContentLabelRemoveRedundant` controls duplicate label
and SVG root text removal and defaults to disabled pending evaluations.
The separate `AIPageContentLabelRemoveRedundantWholeWordMatching` flag
depends on removal and also defaults to disabled. It allows whole words
or phrases to match within longer text, ignoring case. "Password"
matches "Enter your password" and "Password:", but "lock" does not match
"currently unlocked".

For SVGs, the title supplies the label unless ARIA provides one; the
description supplies a fallback label when neither provides text. When
removal is enabled, omit the SVG root's copy of visible text when
extracted child nodes carry it. SVG image captions are included in both
extraction modes and in `GetImageBytes()`.

[CodeHealth] Migrate LimitingAudioQueue to planar_data

This CL replaces uses of the deprecated `AudioBuffer::channel_data()` in
`LimitingAudioQueue` and its unit tests with `planar_data()` and
`planar_channel_cast<const float>()`.

Buffers are always `kSampleFormatPlanarF32`, and `TrimEnd()` already
sizes each planar span to `num_frames` floats, so the output spans
passed to `AudioLimiter` are unchanged. This also removes the
`UNSAFE_TODO()` uses in the tests.

TAG=agy

e02c40e3d01f7c925ee3b3e74e5991d596b920a3
parent
1f86170d549a4ae044012903a2c19de5e720e088 [ diff ] 
[LanguageDetector] Reuse ContentLanguageDetectionDriver per context

Previously, ChromeContentBrowserClient::BindLanguageDetectionDriver()
unconditionally called SetUserData() with a newly constructed
ContentLanguageDetectionDriver. When multiple receivers bound to the
same SupportsUserData context (for example, when both Page Translate's
LanguageDetectionAgent and Blink's AIInterfaceProxy for
window.LanguageDetector bound to the same document), the second binding
destroyed the existing ContentLanguageDetectionDriver and its
mojo::ReceiverSet, disconnecting the first pipe and dropping pending
callbacks.

Update ChromeContentBrowserClient::BindLanguageDetectionDriver() to
reuse the existing ContentLanguageDetectionDriver on context_user_data
when present (matching ChromeContentBrowserClient::BindAIManager()), and
add a browsertest verifying multi-receiver binding on the same context.

Enable low-trust DSE and NTP policy extension blocking by default

In low-trust environments (unmanaged consumer devices), enterprise
policy force-installs and recommendations are abused to lock in search
engine or New Tab Page hijackers.

This CL enables the kBlockPolicyDseNtpOverridesInLowTrust feature flag
by default, activating the end-to-end blocking defense on low-trust
Windows and macOS devices.

TAG=agy

webui: Replace raw HTML in webui_explainer.md with Markdown

Gitiles drops raw HTML silently, so the 6 `<div class="note">` callouts,
the "Mmmm, donuts!" example, inline `<code>`/`<i>`/`<a>` tags and the
bare `<iframe>` in the security section did not render on
chromium.googlesource.com. See
https://gerrit.googlesource.com/gitiles/+/HEAD/Documentation/markdown.md?pli=1#HTML

Convert them to `*** note` blocks, a fenced block, backticks,
underscores and a Markdown link. Remove the `<style>` block (it only
styled the old `.note` divs) and the trailing `<script>` anchor checker;
Gitiles drops both. No content changes.

Revert "Enable bookmark bar drag-and-drop tests on Mac"

This reverts commit 7d9a2c66d30d1e4785aa9207e66a52072d968560.

Reason for revert:
Consistently breaks interactive_ui_tests on builder chrome/ci/mac-rel-ready.
The drag-and-drop simulation fails to complete the in-place item reorder in the
bookmark menu, failing CheckMenuItemBefore() on step 14 / step 17:
- BookmarkBarDragAndDropInteractiveTest.BookmarksDragAndDrop
- BookmarkBarDragAndDropInteractiveTest.BookmarksDragAndDropFromNestedFolder

Failure Link: https://ci.chromium.org/ui/p/chrome/builders/ci/mac-rel-ready/47593/overview

Original change's description:
> Enable bookmark bar drag-and-drop tests on Mac
>
> Enable BookmarkBarDragAndDropInteractiveTest on Mac now that
> ui_controls supports drag loops there.
>
> The post-drop PressButton(kFolderButtonId) step is only needed on X11,
> where MenuController::OnDragDropCompleted() force-closes the menu
> (crbug.com/375959961). On Mac (and other platforms) the menu stays open
> and updates its items in place, so re-pressing the button would instead
> close it.
>

83fcdedf8b222da3fac72067c87e48e8f065de5d [ diff ] 
[Mac] Let window managers resize the browser from the omnibox popup

With the Full WebUI omnibox focused, Moom moved the browser window but
did not resize it. Moom only resizes a window that has a zoom button.
It reads AXZoomButton from AXFocusedWindow, which is the borderless
popup, gets nil, and skips the size write.

OmniboxPopupNSWindow now also proxies AXZoomButton reads to the root
parent window, next to the existing AXPosition/AXSize proxying.

A logged Moom resize showed AXZoomButton is the only attribute it reads
where the popup and the browser window differ. It does not read
AXSubrole, AXGrowArea or the other window buttons.

TAG=agy
CONV=dea59c3f-240a-4dcb-97ab-9f3079863bfb

777bfeaad4f8d08c690b86fd0f06273a837552fc [ diff ] 
components: Decouple proto_extras from Safe Browsing WebUI using check_includes_strict

In clean builds without remote cache (e.g. linux-build-perf-developer
#19905), chrome_content_browser_client.o, browser_view.o, and
web_contents_impl.o were delayed until t = 104.8s waiting on
proto_extras code generation actions (realtimeapi.ostream.h etc.).

However, none of these C++ compilation units include any proto_extras
headers (*.ostream.h or *.to_value.h); they were blocked purely by
GN's order-only dependency (.harddeps) propagation.

With GN CL 27100 rolled into Chromium, setting `check_includes_strict = true`
stops GN from forwarding private `deps`' hard_deps to downstream targets.

This change:
1. components/safe_browsing/content/browser/web_ui/BUILD.gn:
   Sets `check_includes_strict = true`, exposes public headers via
   `public`, and moves private `proto_extras` dependencies to `deps`.
2. components/safe_browsing/core/browser/web_ui/BUILD.gn:
   Sets `check_includes_strict = true` on `:handler` and `:util`,
   keeping `proto_extras` dependencies in private `deps`.

[Extensions] Add site access options to install dialog on Desktop

As part of Click-to-Script Phase 3a work, this CL implements the Desktop
plumbing to present users with site access permissions in the extension
installation dialog.

Demo video:
https://screencast.googleplex.com/cast/NTEzMzU0OTU0MjgzNDE3Nnw3YzJiOTkyZS1iOQ

It removes the legacy "Grant access to all requested sites" checkbox
from the dialog's extra view and adds a new "Allow site access" section
containing two radio buttons: "When you click on the extension" (which
is selected by default) and "Always on all sites”. It also updates
ExtensionInstallDialogView::OnDialogAccepted() to check the radio button
state and return ACCEPTED_WITH_WITHHELD_PERMISSIONS or ACCEPTED
accordingly.

This change is flag-guarded behind the
kExtensionInstallSiteAccessOptions flag. The Desktop Android plumbing
and UX polish will be implemented in a follow-up CL.

Fri Oct 02 23:15:41 2026
tree
b804b6f5cf6cc6ba080f290c601052f2df928b0d
parent
5fce2f866e6f286ae95c01df6e62699e5248c456 [ diff ] 
[composebox] Do not surface upload errors for unattached tokens

Prior to crrev/c/8283473, errorMessage was initialized to null and only
assigned if file existed in attachedContext. For unattached or
background tokens, updateFileStatus() returned {file: null,
errorMessage: null}. After crrev/c/8283473, updateFileStatus() returned
a non-null errorMessage even when file was null.
onContextualInputStatusChanged() checks if (errorMessage) and sets
this.errorMessage = errorMessage, displaying an error scrim in the
composebox for an unattached file.

This broke the ChromeOS Google Lens side panel flow: clicking "Learn
more about AI and privacy" in the onboarding tooltip calls
OpenOnboardingHelpUi(), opening a new foreground tab. The tab switch
triggers session expiration, dispatching
ContextUploadStatus::kUploadExpired for background file tokens. Because
the ChromeOS side panel WebUI remains alive across tab navigations, it
received kUploadExpired for an unattached token, causing the composebox
to display "File Upload has expired and been removed" incorrectly

This CL restores the condition that errorMessage is initialized to null
and only computed when the token belongs to an attached file in
attachedContext.

0665e18cbc17d5ec0a809ba8c4d88ebd3baa16fa [ diff ] 
[ios][ntp] Fix MVT height calculation in redesign bottom sheet

In the New Tab Page redesign bottom sheet, Most Visited Tiles (MVT) were
clipped at the top because header height and feed scroll insets were
calculated from stale wrapper container bounds before the collection
view finished its layout pass.

This CL:
1. Updates `MostVisitedContainerHeight` to directly inspect the inner
   `MostVisitedTilesCollectionView.contentSize.height` plus bottom
   padding, falling back to system layout fitting or intrinsic size.
2. Passes `MostVisitedTilesCollectionView` to
   `NewTabPageBottomSheetViewController` so bottom sheet insets can
   accurately reflect the collection view's content size.
3. Updates `NewTabPageRedesignViewController` to trigger
   `[_bottomSheetViewController updateFeedInsets]` when MVT content
   size changes via `onContentSizeChanged`.
4. Fixes child view controller hierarchy management for Magic Stack and
   Feed Top Section by determining target parent controllers before
   embedding, preventing `UIViewControllerHierarchyInconsistency`
   exceptions during compact/regular transitions.

[ChroMenu GlowUp] Enable bookmarks drag/drop (2/6)

Introduce AppMenuDragAndDropDelegate and
AppMenuActionItem::kDragAndDropDelegateKey so dynamic submenus in
ActionAppMenu can handle drag-and-drop operations via
actions::BaseAction* rather than views::MenuItemView*.

ActionAppMenu resolves MenuItemViews to their corresponding BaseActions,
walks the action ancestor chain to locate the delegate, and forwards
views::MenuDelegate drag-and-drop calls.

[Android] Replace --shards with -j/--jobs to set JUnit test worker count

LocalMachineJunitTestRun previously used the CLI argument --shards to
configure the number of concurrent worker processes in the test pool.
This naming was confusing because --shards does not partition tests into
shards; test batches generated by GroupTests are referred to as shards
(and filtered via --shard-filter), while bot-level sharding uses
--test-launcher-total-shards.

Replace --shards with -j / --jobs to configure worker concurrency.

tree
2a6dc822b02937eea173d4b9cda59af09071bf90
parent
1466a02701e20d76b6e260f04067c49ccf5862d3 [ diff ] 
proto_extras: Parallelize to_value and ostream generation

In proto_extras.gni, proto_library(_ostream_target_name) previously
listed `deps = [ ":$_to_value_target_name" ]`. In proto_library.gni,
items in `invoker.deps` cause the action (`${target_name}_gen`) to
depend on `${_dep}_gen`.

As a result, the protoc action for stream operator generation
unnecessarily waited for the to_value protoc action to finish. For
example, in Safe Browsing, realtimeapi_proto_extras_ostream_gen (14.5s)
waited for realtimeapi_proto_extras_to_value_gen (10.7s), causing a
25.2s serial delay.

Because stream operator code generation only reads .proto files and
does not depend on the generated to_value header or C++ code during
protoc execution, we can move the dependency to `link_public_deps`.
This allows the two code generation actions to run in parallel while
preserving all C++ compilation and link dependencies.

parent
a87e0d8d162fd2d73448c427294ebcc31e35533d [ diff ] 
hid: Deep-link to Android HID Special App Access

On Android 17+, accessing WebHID devices requires the system-level
Special App Access permission (android.permission.ACCESS_HID).
Previously, Chrome displayed a modal dialog with an "OK" button
instructing users to navigate manually through Android Settings.

With the platform intent android.settings.MANAGE_APP_ACCESS_HID added to
Android Settings, Chrome can now deep-link users directly to Chrome's
HID access toggle page.

This CL updates HidChooserDialog to detect whether
android.settings.MANAGE_APP_ACCESS_HID can be resolved on the device at
runtime via PackageManagerUtils.canResolveActivity(). If resolvable, the
dialog presents a "Settings" button that starts the settings intent with
Chrome's package URI alongside a "Cancel" button. If unresolvable (e.g.
on development builds without the updated Settings activity), it
gracefully falls back to the existing informational dialog with an "OK"
button.

2919f850d028bec8ebd11c4af0b4a62bfc78e570 [ diff ] 
[CRD Linux] Order chrome-remote-desktop@ after systemd-user-sessions

If the single-process host starts before user logins are allowed (e.g.
while goobuntu-updater holds systemd-user-sessions.service at boot),
pam_systemd times out waiting for user@<uid>.service and the session is
left without XDG_RUNTIME_DIR/XDG_SESSION_ID, breaking `systemctl --user`
and the session D-Bus.

Add After=systemd-user-sessions.service, matching gdm.service,
getty@.service and chrome-remote-desktop-x11-session@.service.

3a72c04989bdf3ef72bddb6a6599e8b1829d46d6
parent
f74b0d578431d71bc60767f72d0e10fcafa108ee [ diff ] 
[Actor] Replace ActorSurfaceId with ActorSurfaceHandle

Introduces ActorSurfaceHandle as a lightweight value handle that
resolves to a live ActorSurface* via Get(), backed by an internal
process-wide lookup table managed by ActorSurface's constructor and
destructor. This matches the tabs::TabHandle pattern and allows callers
to safely resolve a surface across asynchronous gaps without plumbing
ActorSurfaceRegistry everywhere.

ActorSurfaceRegistry remains the profile-scoped owner of ActorSurface
instances and validates that tabs passed to GetOrCreateForTab are live
and belong to the registry's BrowserContext. Headless surface handles
use negative values during migration (kUseTabHandleAsSurfaceHandle)
so they remain disjoint from positive TabHandle values.

Support --bypass-skia-gold-functionality in variations smoke tests

The skia_gold_test mixin in infra/config/targets/mixins.star defines
--bypass-skia-gold-functionality as an emergency toggle to disable Skia
Gold image comparisons across CI/CQ during Skia Gold service outages
(such as crrev.com/c/8494678 for crbug.com/skia/568081757).

Because variations_desktop_smoke_tests uses the skia_gold_test mixin,
enabling --bypass-skia-gold-functionality in mixins.star passes the flag
to pytest. Without registering the option in chrome/test/variations,
pytest fails during argument parsing with an unrecognized argument error
and SkiaGoldProperties does not receive
bypass_skia_gold_functionality=True.

Register --bypass-skia-gold-functionality in pytest_addoption in
chrome/test/variations/conftest.py and parse it in _get_skia_gold_args()
in chrome/test/variations/fixtures/skia_gold.py so that pytest accepts
the flag and SkiaGoldSession.RunComparison() bypasses goldctl
comparisons when Skia Gold is disabled.

Bug: 568511686, 568508314, 568492608, 568445507, 567295975

[Extensions] WebRequest: Check initiator against user host restrictions

`CanExtensionAccessURLInternal()` checked request initiators against
enterprise policy-blocked hosts, but not against user-restricted sites
("block all extensions on this site"). The initiator host-permission
check that normally covers user restrictions is skipped for navigation
requests (crbug.com/41433450) and for `DO_NOT_CHECK_HOST`, so
navigations (e.g. iframes, form POSTs) initiated by a user-restricted
site to an allowed site were visible to, and modifiable by, extensions.
Additionally, opaque initiators (e.g. sandboxed frames) were not
resolved to their precursor origin for these checks.

Check the initiator (or its precursor, if opaque) against both
policy-blocked and user-restricted hosts upfront, before any per-mode
host-permission logic. This matches existing enterprise policy behavior.

Behavior changes:
- Navigations initiated by a user-restricted site (including outbound
  link clicks to allowed sites) are no longer visible to webRequest or
  matched by declarativeNetRequest.
- declarativeNetRequest rules that don't require host permissions
  (e.g. block) no longer apply to requests initiated by user-restricted
  sites.
- Opaque-origin initiators whose precursor is policy-blocked are now
  also denied.

Fixed: 517435285

[chromecast] Add ProGuard config exclusion to dist_aar

`dist_aar` currently merges all transitive `proguard_configs` into the
output AAR's `proguard.txt`, including APK-level ProGuard configurations
such as `//base/android/proguard/chromium_apk.flags`.

`chromium_apk.flags` contains whole-program APK optimization directives
(such as `-assumenosideeffects class android.**, java.**` and
`-repackageclasses`) intended only for top-level APK targets, not
library AARs. When `cast_browser.aar` is imported as a library AAR into
downstream build pipelines that run Jetifier and whole-program
shrinking, Jetifier rewrites the `android.**` pattern in
`chromium_apk.flags` into invalid `-assumenosideeffects` rules without
member bodies, failing the downstream ProGuard configuration parser.

Add `proguard_excluded_patterns` (`--proguard-excluded-globs`) to the
`dist_aar` template and `dist_aar.py` script, and exclude
`*/chromium_apk.flags` in `cast_browser_dist_aar`.

Bug: 461540377, 408056924, 548679569
Test: autoninja -C out/atv_x86_eng cast_browser_dist_aar
Test: Verified chromium_apk.flags is excluded from AAR proguard.txt

[chromecast] Add background_task_scheduler_java to cast_browser_dist_aar

`libcast_browser_android.so` links native `background_task_scheduler`
code, causing `cast_browser_jni_registration` to generate JNI bindings
referencing `TaskInfoBridge` in package
`org.chromium.components.background_task_scheduler.internal`.
However, `background_task_scheduler_java` was not included in the
dependencies of `cast_browser_dist_aar`, omitting `TaskInfoBridge.class`
from `cast_browser.aar`'s `classes.jar` and causing unresolved class
reference errors during downstream R8/ProGuard whole-program linking.

Add
`//components/background_task_scheduler:background_task_scheduler_java`
to `cast_browser_dist_aar` `deps` so `TaskInfoBridge` and its
associated Java classes are packaged in `cast_browser.aar`.

Bug: 548679569
Test: autoninja -C out/atv_x86_eng cast_browser_dist_aar
Test: Verified TaskInfoBridge.class is present in cast_browser.aar

[chromecast] Switch to component FFmpeg on Android

Without `is_component_ffmpeg = true`, non-component Android Cast builds
statically link FFmpeg into `libcast_browser_android.so` instead of
producing a standalone `libffmpeg.so` in `cast_browser.aar`.

When `cast_browser.aar` is packaged into an Android receiver APK
alongside other native libraries that dynamically load `libffmpeg.so`
via `dlopen`/`dlsym`, the statically linked FFmpeg symbols exported by
`libcast_browser_android.so` populate the global symbol namespace first
and shadow `dlsym` lookups against `libffmpeg.so`. Additionally,
`cast_browser_dist_aar` only packages `libffmpeg.so` into the AAR when
`is_component_ffmpeg` is enabled.

Setting `is_component_ffmpeg = true` in
`chromecast/build/args/config/android.gni` builds FFmpeg as a separate
shared library (`libffmpeg.so`) and packages it in `cast_browser.aar`,
resolving the missing library and symbol shadowing issues.

Bug: 562120442, 565452930, 548679569
Test: autoninja -C out/atv_x86_eng cast_browser_dist_aar
Test: Verified jni/*/libffmpeg.so is packaged in cast_browser.aar

PaintTiming: Merge TextElementTiming into ElementTiming

Follow-up to crrev.com/c/8382996, this finishes merging image and text
element timing into a single class. Some details:
 - The intersection logic is moved to ElementTiming, allowing
   ElementTimingUtils to be deleted. We may need to add back a utils
   or base class when we split off ContainerTiming, but let's wait on
   that.
 - I audited the merged class and unified and removed redundant checks
   where possible, e.g. removing tests for filtering on shadow tree or
   is-element when that filter has already been applied.
 - NotifyImageRemoved is removed in favor of OnImageRemoved (inherited
   from PaintTimingClient).

Container Timing cleanup and changes: this unifies the behavior of image
and text container timing integration by:
 1. Sending all "needed" elements (elementtiming or containertiming) to
    ContainerTiming when the feature is enabled. We were doing this for
    text, but not images. Let's be consistent and let ContainerTiming
    do the filtering (moving towards decoupling these features).
 2. Don't guard on CanReportToContainerTiming() in ElementTiming. We
    were inconsistent here too: text guarded on this, but images
    didn't. This should be ContainerTiming's responsibility (it already
    checks before emitting), and early filtering means the total area
    doesn't get updated, which IMO is a bug.

Bug: 454082773, 535432431, 559075199

Extensions: Guard Android extension UI targets with enable_extensions_core

Use enable_extensions_core instead of is_desktop_android to guard build
targets under //c/b/ui/android/extensions/side_panel/ and
//c/b/ui/android/extensions/windowing/.

These targets depend on //chrome/browser/extensions, which asserts
enable_extensions_core. Using enable_extensions_core aligns their build
conditions with their dependencies and with other Android extension UI
targets.

[css-mixins] Invalidate shadow DOM mixin consumers

Changing a mixin in an outer tree scope only rebuilt RuleSets in that
scope. Descendant shadow styles that applied the inherited mixin could
remain flattened against the old definition.

Propagate mixin changes along paths to dependent shadow scopes and
refresh intermediate scope identities so local mixins cannot hide an
inherited change. Preserve the existing identity when the effective
mixin map is unchanged to avoid rebuilding dependent RuleSets after
unrelated stylesheet updates.

CSSOM mutation can modify a mixin in place without changing its rule
pointer. Track a monotonic revision for each stylesheet's extracted
mixins so every collection sharing a constructable stylesheet observes
the mutation independently.

Add coverage for direct and nested shadow consumers, including a shared
constructable stylesheet adopted into sibling scopes. Also expose the
dependent-scope invalidation phase as a trace metric for the mixin
invalidation perf tests.
